```yaml
lines:
  - id: f8863.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero amount (an entry of -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: f8863.eligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You may claim an education credit: qualified education expenses were paid, and your filing status is not married filing separately"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8863.paid, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
    note: "Open point: i8863 'Who cannot claim a credit' lists five conditions. 1. Claimed as a dependent on another person's return. 2. Married filing separately. 3. Nonresident alien without an election. 4. MAGI at or above the limit. 5. Student TIN not issued by the due date. The supplied interface holds no return-level fact for item 1. For item 3, scope.nonresident does not record whether the election was made. Choice: this line encodes only the expenses-paid gate and item 2. The MAGI limit is applied through lines 4, 15 and 17-style proration. TIN and per-student conditions are left to the Part III fields."

  - id: f8863.h_l30_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total of Parts III, line 30, for students on the return (you and your spouse)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.edu_line30
      where: {op: test, eq: [person.edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
    note: "Open point: the IRS text does not say which entries carry line 30. Choice: count only students for whom lines 27-30 are completed (person.edu_aoc). The form says not to complete line 31 for those students."

  - id: f8863.h_l30_dep
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total of Parts III, line 30, for dependent students"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.edu_line30
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_aoc, {const: true}]}

  - id: f8863.h_l30_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total of all amounts from all Parts III, line 30"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8863.h_l30_person, f8863.h_l30_dep]}
    refuses: [f8863.ban]
    note: "Open point: f8863.ban covers two cases. One is a prior AOC disallowance that requires Form 8862. The other is a 2- or 10-year ban. Choice: the tool refuses here and names Form 8862. It does not compute the credit."

  - id: f8863.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "After completing Part III for each student, enter the total of all amounts from all Parts III, line 30"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8863.eligible, {const: true}]}
          then: f8863.h_l30_total
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8863.eligible, {const: false}]}
          then: f8863.h_zero

  - id: f8863.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Enter $180,000 if married filing jointly; or $90,000 if single, head of household, or qualifying surviving spouse"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: edu.magi_limit}

  - id: f8863.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b. But if you're filing Form 2555 or 4563, or you're excluding income from Puerto Rico, see Pub. 970 for the amount to enter instead"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}
    refuses: [sa.excluded_income]

  - id: f8863.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Subtract line 3 from line 2. If zero or less, stop; you can't take any education credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8863.line2, take: f8863.line3}
    note: "STOP when line 4 is zero: line 7 prorates by line 4 and so becomes zero. Line 15 uses the same MAGI and limit, so lines 15 and 18 are also zero. No education credit results."

  - id: f8863.h_go
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 4 is more than zero (the form continues past line 4)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f8863.line4, {const: 0}]}

  - id: f8863.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "Enter $20,000 if married filing jointly; or $10,000 if single, head of household, or qualifying surviving spouse"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: edu.phaseout_range}
    shown_when: f8863.h_go

  - id: f8863.line7
    printed: "7"
    label: "Multiply line 1 by line 6 (line 6: if line 4 is equal to or more than line 5, 1.000; otherwise line 4 divided by line 5, rounded to at least three places)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: prorate, amount: f8863.line1, part: f8863.line4, whole: f8863.line5, places: 3}
    shown_when: f8863.h_go
    note: "Open point: line 6 is rounded 'to at least three places'. Choice: exactly three decimal places."

  - id: f8863.h_kid_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of taxpayer entries meeting the age, support and parent conditions (instructions for line 7, conditions 1 and 2)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [person.role, {const: "taxpayer"}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_kid, {const: true}]}
    note: "Open point: person.edu_kid is taken to be the per-person result of the instructions' conditions 1(a, b or c) and 2. Condition 3, not filing jointly, is tested on the box line itself."

  - id: f8863.no_refund
    printed: "7 (box)"
    label: "If you were under age 24 at the end of the year and meet the conditions described in the instructions, you can't take the refundable American opportunity credit; skip line 8, enter the amount from line 7 on line 9, and check this box"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:

        - {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}

  - id: f8863.h_show8
    printed: "(helper)"

  - id: f8863.line8

    expr:

Second transcription's note: Open point: i8863 'Who cannot claim a credit' lists five conditions. 1. Claimed as a dependent on another person's return. 2. Married filing separately. 3. Nonresident alien without an election. 4. MAGI at or above the limit. 5. Student TIN not issued by the due date. The supplied interface holds no return-level fact for item 1. For item 3, scope.nonresident does not record whether the election was made. Choice: this line encodes only the expenses-paid gate and item 2. The MAGI limit is applied through lines 4, 15 and 17-style proration. TIN and per-student conditions are left to the Part III fields.

Ruling: open
Reviewed in: pass 573118875700
